Let's design a robust "soft constrained multiplicative search" (SCMS):
- Work with a normalized sum1 length n.
- At each iteration:
  * c = conv(a,a), S=1.
  * Compute weights p over convolution coefficients. Several variants:
      - Rank-based: p_m = 0 for c_m < τ, else (c_m-τ)/sum, with τ = M - Δ M. Δ maybe 0.05M or adapt.
      - Power: p_m = c_m^q / sum (q large emphasizes peaks). q maybe 50.
      - Smooth max softmax: p_m = exp(β(c_m-M))/sum with β adaptive.
  * Compute v_i = cross_corr(a,p) (h_i =∂ logsumexp/2).
  * Project gradient: d_i = v_i - mean(v_i) (sum zero).
  * Update a = a * exp(-lr * d_i / scale) or additive a - lr*d/scale. Normalize. Use line search/backtracking to ensure score decreases (or at least regularized objective).
- Periodically recompute exact score and keep best.

This is a kind of mirror descent for min logsumexp on simplex. Since logsumexp is convex in c but c bilinear in a; still heuristic.

The choice of p matters. For uniform a, p based on c = triangular; v_i = crosscorr(a,p). p triangular centered; a uniform => v_i = (1/n)*∑_j p_{i+j}. This is a triangular smoothing, maximum center. d higher center, update decreases center mass. Good. Over iterations, a may become anti-symmetric/edge heavy.

We can tune weight exponent. For peak reduction, p should emphasize top few but not too concentrated (stationary). Use p_m = top-W active with weights proportional to c_m - (M - δ). δ choose maybe M/K (K active). We can adapt K from n: top Q e.g. 10%, or all with c > T threshold where T=(M*M?)? Actually target ratio. We can use p_m = max(0, c_m - T) / sum with T = current_score? If T = (target/ (2n))? But target unknown. Use T = M*(1-ε). Or T = current_M/2? Use logsumexp with β = 1/(M - T) equivalent. We can set T = M*(1 - gap), gap=0.1. That includes top 10% peak region. For uniform triangular, c values near center; selecting top 10% maybe good.

Update step: Multiplicative update a_i ← a_i * (1 - η d_i / (max|d|)) can make zeros. Use softplus? Additive with clipping maybe. Multiplicative can be more scale-invariant; if an a_i=0, stays0. If we need create zeros, okay. But if not enough, can add small ε before update? To avoid all zeros, add small floor (e.g., 1e-12) for updates? But if optimal has zeros, floor okay. Mirror search function:
```
def mirror_search(a, max_iters, time_limit, start_iter=0, base_label='mirror'):
    a = normalize clip
    best_a=a; best_sc=score(a)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(hash(label))
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time > limit: break
       c=self_conv(a); M=max(c)
       # choose p adaptively
       # cycle through modes/gaps
       gap = 0.05 * (it//50 % 5+1)?
       p = active_weights(c,...)
       for lr in lrs:
          a_try=mirror(a,p,lr)
          sc=score(a_try)
          if sc < best_sc* (1-1e-8): accept? But could accept small improvements repeatedly.
       if no improvement: perturb
    return best_a,best_sc
```

Let's plan low-dim Fourier optimizer concretely:
Parameter vector θ of length P for Fourier series:
f(x) = 1 + ∑_{k=1}^P [α_k cos(2πk x) + β_k sin(2πk x)].
Need f≥0. We can enforce during optimization by projecting: f_raw = 1 + coeffs; clip to small epsilon (e.g., 1e-6) and normalize to sum1 over discrete grid? But clipping breaks Fourier structure; okay as heuristic. Alternatively parameterize f=1+max_amp * tanh coefficients? For simplicity, generate f positive by `f = 1 + scale * sum( cos/sin ) + eps`; if any negative, reduce scale or add offset. In optimizer, we can allow variables, compute f_pos = max(f, 0), normalize. The objective is smooth except at zeros; okay.

How to optimize? Use random search + Nelder-Mead? The objective as a function of coefficients is smooth if no clip. We can implement finite-difference gradient using FFT efficient? High-dim mirror_search details:
```
def mirror_search(a, time_limit, label, rng):
   a=clip normalize
   best_a=a; best_sc=score(a)
   it=0
   no_improve=0
   while time < time_limit:
      c=self_conv(a)
      M=c.max()
      cur_sc=2*n*M  # since sum1
      if cur_sc < best_sc: update
      # choose p
      cycle = it % 6
      if cycle in (0,1,2): p = power normalize (c/M)^q with q [8,32,128]
      elif cycle in (3,4,5): p = top_gap M-gap where gap [0.02,0.1,0.3]M
      # update candidates
      v=cross_corr(a,p)
      d=v - np.mean(v)
      scale=max(abs(d)); if scale<=tiny: perturb
      lrs = [0.05,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0]
      best_a_try=None; best_try_sc=cur_sc
      for lr in lrs:
          a_try = a * exp(-lr*d/scale)
          a_try = max(a_try,0); normalize
          sc=score(a_try)
          if sc < best_try_sc: best_try...
      if best_try_sc < cur_sc:
          a=best_try; no_improve=0
      elif best_try_sc < best_sc*1.01 and it%5==0: maybe accept best_try? no
      else:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve > 20:
              # perturb from best_a
              a = best_a.copy()
              # random Fourier smooth noise
              noise=smooth_random(n, rng, sigma=0.2)
              a = best_a * exp(0.2*noise)
              a=normalize
              no_improve=0
      it+=1
```

Perturbation: Generate smooth noise:
```
def smooth_noise(n, rng, sigma=0.2, smooth=32):
    z=rng.standard_normal(n)
    # simple moving avg length smooth
    k=max(1, n//50)
    z=np.convolve(z, np.ones(k)/k, mode='same')
    z -= z.mean()
    z /= (z.std()+1e-12)
    return z*sigma
```

```
       amt=t*max(0,a[i]); a_try=a.copy(); a_try[i]-=amt; a_try[j]+=amt; normalize; score
```

```
def build_fourier_basis(n, max_freq, use_monomials=True):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   cols=[]
   if use_monomials:
      cols.append(x-0.5)
      cols.append((x-0.5)**2 - 1/(12)) # maybe centered
      cols.append((x-0.5)**3?)
   for k in range(1,max_freq+1):
      cols.append(np.cos(2πkx))
      cols.append(np.sin(2πkx))
   return np.column_stack(cols)
```

```
def random_fourier_init(n, rng, max_amp=0.7, freq=8):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   amp=rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)
   phases... f=1

      f += scale*(rng.uniform(-1,1)*np.cos(2πkx)+rng.uniform(-1,1)*np.sin(2πkx))
```

```
n=len(a); M=2*n-1; L=next_pow2(M)
A=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)
return c[:M]
```
For long a float64.
- `score_sequence(a)`:
```
S = float(np.sum(a))
if S < 1e-15: return np.inf
c = self_conv(a)
maxc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
return float(2*len(a)*maxc/(S*S))
```
- `normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)

s=a.sum()
if s<=1e-15: return np.ones_like(a)/len(a)
return a/s
```
For internal sum=1; final can return normalized sum=1.

- `cross_corr(a,p)`:
```
n=len(a); M2=len(p) # 2n-1
# p reversed
q=p[::-1]
L=next_pow2(len(a)+len(q)-1)
A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); Q=np.fft.rfft(q,L)
r=np.fft.irfft(A*Q, L)
# h_j = r[2n-2-j]
return r[2*n-2::-1][:n]
```
Check r length maybe less if L small? L≥ len(a)+len(q)-1, yes. Good.

Could use `r = np.asarray(r, dtype=np.float64)`; small negative imaginary? real.

- `make_peak_weights(c, mode, param)`:
```
def exponential_weights(c, q):
    M=float(c.max())
    if M<=1e-300: return np.full(c.size, 1.0/c.size)
    cn=c/M
    p=cn**q
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=1e-300: p=cn
    s=p.sum(); return p/s
def gap_weights(c, gap):
    M=float(c.max()); thr=M*(1-gap)
    p=np.maximum(c-thr,0.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=1e-300: p=np.ones(c.size); s=p.size
    return p/s
```
Could `q` float not integer; `cn**q` fine (cn≥0). For q large, underflow ok.

- `smooth_noise(n, rng, sigma, smooth_frac)`:
```
z=rng.standard_normal(n)
k=max(3, int(n*smooth_frac))
z=np.convolve(z, np.ones(k, dtype=float)/k, mode='same')
z-=z.mean(); sd=z.std(); if sd>0: z/=sd
return z*sigma
```

- `mirror_search(a, max_time_s, rng, label, allow_slack=0.01, use_shave=True)`:

Pseudo:
```
def mirror_search(a, deadline, rng, label, allow_slack=0.01):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    n=len(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_sequence(a)
    lrs=[0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8]
    q_cycle=[5,10,20,40,80]
    gap_cycle=[0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4]
    it=0; no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
       c=self_conv(a); S=float(a.sum()); M=float(c.max()); cur=2*n*M/(S*S)
       if cur < best_sc - 1e-12: update
       # choose mode
       phase=it % 8
       if phase <4:
          p=exponential_weights(c, q_cycle[phase])
       else:
          p=gap_weights(c, gap_cycle[phase-4])
       v=cross_corr(a,p); d=v-v.mean(); scale=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
       accepted=False
       if scale>1e-18:
          best_cand=None; best_cand_sc=None
          for lr in lrs:
             if lr*scale > 3: continue
             a_try=a*np.exp(-lr*(d/scale))
             a_try=np.maximum(a_try,0.0); a_try=normalize_nonneg(a_try)
             sc=score_sequence(a_try)
             if sc < (best_cand_sc if best_cand_sc is not None else 1e300) and sc < cur + allow_slack:
                 best_cand_sc=sc; best_cand=a_try
          if best_cand is not None:
             if best_cand_sc < cur - 1e-12: no_improve=0
             elif no_improve < 5: no_improve +=1
             else: no_improve+=1
             a=best_cand; accepted=True
       if not accepted:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve > 15:
              # perturb from best or random Fourier
              base=best_a.copy()
              noise=smooth_noise(n,rng,0.25,0.02)
              a=base*np.exp(noise); a=normalize_nonneg(a)
              # maybe peak shave
              v2=cross_corr(a, gap_weights(self_conv(a),0.05)); d2=v2-v2.mean(); i=np.argmax(d2); j=np.argmin(d2)
              for t in [0.05,0.1,0.2]:
                 amt=t*max(0.0,a[i])
                 ap=a.copy(); ap[i]-=amt; ap[j]+=amt; ap=normalize_nonneg(ap); sc=score_sequence(ap)
                 if sc<best_sc: best...
              if sc not improve, a maybe base? We can keep perturbed to explore.
              no_improve=0
       it+=1
       if it%20==0: print progress
    # final polish: if time, small lrs on best
    if time.monotonic() < deadline-5:
       a=best_a
       for small_lrs in [0.01,0.02,0.05]: ... maybe one pass
    return best_a,best_sc
```
Need be careful if best_a changes but a diverges; after many no improve, reset a=best_a? In perturbation, we set a perturbed from best; if it doesn't improve, next iterations continue from perturbed or reset? We can keep perturbed to escape. But if it raises score, line search may choose weird. We can instead if no improve for 15, set `a = best_a copy`, perturb mild, and continue; if perturbed score >best, still okay for exploration. Track best exact.

```
def build_fourier_basis(n, max_freq, use_poly=True):
    x=np.linspace(0.1,1.0,n)? Should grid correspond to sequence positions? The evaluator sequence index 0..n-1; continuous x = i/(n-1) or i/n? For convolution continuous mapping x=i/(n-1)? Usually i/n. Use x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n). Endpoints included; fine.
    cols=[]
    if use_poly:
       xc=x-0.5
       cols.append(xc)
       cols.append(xc*xc - 1.0/12.0)
       cols.append(xc*xc*xc)
    for k in range(1,max_freq+1):
       cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x))
       cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*k*x))
    return np.vstack if one else column_stack
```

```
x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
f=np.ones(n)
for k in range(1,max_freq+1):
    amp=rng.uniform(0.1, amp_max)/(k**0.6)
    phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
    f += amp*np.cos(2πkx+phase)
    amp2=rng.uniform(0.1, amp_max)/(k**0.6)
    phase2=rng.uniform(0,2π)
    f += amp2*np.sin(2πkx+phase2)
return normalize_nonneg(f)
```

```
def sine_init(n, amp1=0.5, amp4=-0.25, amp6=0.15, amp2=0.2):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   f=1+amp1*np.sin(2πx)+amp2*np.sin(4πx)+amp3*np.sin(6πx)+...
   return normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(f,1e-8))
```

```
def full_lbfgs(a0,beta,maxiter,maxfun,gtol=1e-10):
    n=len(a0)
    bounds=[(0.0,1000.0) for _ in range(n)]
    res=minimize(full_lse_obj, a0, jac=full_lse_grad, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':gtol})
    x=np.clip(res.x,0,1000); sc=score_sequence(x)
    return x,sc
```

```python
"""
Search program for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

Strategy:
- Normalize candidates to sum 1 (objective is scale invariant).
- Use FFT-based exact convolution for candidate scores.
- Run a rapid low-resolution mirror-descent / peak-weighted optimization phase.
- Run a low-dimensional Fourier exponential-parameter L-BFGS phase to find good macro shapes.
- Interpolate / re-instantiate good shapes at higher resolution.
- Run a high-resolution mirror/peak-shaving local search.
- Finish with a few short L-BFGS polishes on a log-sum-exp smoothing of the true max.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def next_pow2(v):
    """Smallest power of two >= v."""
    v = int(v)
    if v <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (v - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    """Clip to [0, inf), make sum 1. Fallback uniform if all zero."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, np.inf)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, a.size)
    return a / s


def self_conv(a):
    """Exact linear self-convolution via FFT."""
    n = len(a)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    L = next_pow2(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)
    return c[:m]


def score_sequence(a):
    """Exact objective score, with safe scale handling."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    c = self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mx = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * len(a) * mx / (S * S))


def cross_corr(a, p):
    """
    For a length n, p length 2n-1, return h_j = sum_i a_i p_{i+j}.
    This is 1/2 of the gradient of LSE of conv(a,a) when p is the softmax weights.
    """
    n = len(a)
    q = p[::-1]
    L = next_pow2(len(a) + len(q) - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    Q = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
    r = np.fft.irfft(A * Q, L)
    return r[2 * n - 2 :: -1][:n]


def exponential_peak_weights(c, q):
    """Weights p_m proportional to (c_m/max(c))^q."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    M = float(c.max())
    if M <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(c.shape, 1.0 / c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    cn = np.maximum(c, 0.0) / M
    p = cn ** float(q)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p = cn
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            return np.full(c.shape, 1.0 / c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return p / s


def gap_peak_weights(c, gap):
    """Weights p_m proportional to max(c_m - (1-gap)max(c), 0)."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    M = float(c.max())
    if M <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(c.shape, 1.0 / c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    thr = M * (1.0 - gap)
    p = np.maximum(c - thr, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(c.shape, 1.0 / c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return p / s


def smooth_noise(n, rng, sigma=0.25, smooth_frac=0.02):
    """Smooth zero-mean noise of given sigma."""
    z = rng.standard_normal(n)
    k = int(max(3, min(n, max(3, n * smooth_frac))))
    ker = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k)
    z = np.convolve(z, ker, mode="same")
    z -= float(z.mean())
    sd = float(z.std())
    if sd > 1e-15:
        z = z / sd
    return z * float(sigma)


def random_fourier_dense(n, rng, max_freq=8, amp_max=0.85):
    """Random positive smooth shape from 1 + small Fourier sum."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, max_freq + 1):
        ak = rng.uniform(0.05, amp_max) / (float(k) ** 0.55)
        pk = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        f += ak * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + pk)
        bk = rng.uniform(0.05, amp_max) / (float(k) ** 0.55)
        qk = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        f += bk * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + qk)
    return normalize_nonneg(f)


def sine_shaped_init(n, a1=0.45, a2=-0.28, a3=0.15, a4=0.10):
    """Deterministic asymmetric sine shape; often a good macro seed."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    f = (
        1.0
        + a1 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
        + a2 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x)
        + a3 * np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * x)
        + a4 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    )
    return normalize_nonneg(f)


def interpolate_sequence(a, n_new):
    """Linear interpolation of a sequence to n_new points, normalized."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64) / n_new
    if a.size == n_new:
        return a.copy()
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    y = np.interp(xn, xo, np.maximum(a, 0.0))
    return normalize_nonneg(y)


def update_best(best_a, best_sc, a, sc, label):
    """Return updated best if candidate is clearly better."""
    if a is None:
        return best_a, best_sc
    try:
        a_arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a_arr.size == 0:
            return best_a, best_sc
        if not (np.all(np.isfinite(a_arr))):
            return best_a, best_sc
        a_arr = np.clip(a_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if a_arr.sum() <= 1e-15:
            return best_a, best_sc
        if not (math.isfinite(float(sc))):
            return best_a, best_sc
        if float(sc) < float(best_sc) - 1e-12:
            print(
                "  [best] {}: {:.10f} (n={})".format(label, float(sc), a_arr.size),
                flush=True,
            )
            return a_arr.copy(), float(sc)
    except Exception:
        return best_a, best_sc
    return best_a, best_sc


def mirror_search(a0, deadline, rng, label, allow_slack=0.01, lr_scale=1.0):
    """
    Peak-weighted mirror descent on the probability simplex.
    Uses p_m derived from current conv peak, gradient v = cross_corr(a,p),
    then multiplicative update a <- a * exp(-lr*(v-mean(v))/scale).
    """
    a = normalize_nonneg(a0).astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    n = len(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_sequence(a)
    print(
        "  mirror {} start: {:.8f} n={}".format(label, best_sc, n),
        flush=True,
    )

    base_lrs = [0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40, 0.80, 1.40]
    lrs = [lr_scale * x for x in base_lrs]

    q_cycle = [5, 12, 30, 80]
    gap_cycle = [0.02, 0.06, 0.15, 0.35]

    it = 0
    it_since_best = 0

    while time.monotonic() < max(1.0, deadline - 2.0):
        c = self_conv(a)
        S = float(a.sum())
        if S <= 1e-15:
            a = np.ones(n) / n
            continue
        M = float(c.max())
        cur_sc = float(2 * n * M / (S * S))

        if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = cur_sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            it_since_best = 0

        phase = it % 8
        if phase < 4:
            p = exponential_peak_weights(c, q_cycle[phase])
        else:
            p = gap_peak_weights(c, gap_cycle[phase - 4])

        v = cross_corr(a, p)
        d = v - float(v.mean())
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))

        accepted = False
        if scale > 1e-18:
            chosen = None
            chosen_sc = 1e300
            for lr in lrs:
                if lr <= 0.0:
                    continue
                if lr * scale > 6.0:
                    continue
                a_try = a * np.exp(-lr * d / scale)
                a_try = np.maximum(a_try, 0.0)
                a_try = normalize_nonneg(a_try)
                try:
                    sc_try = score_sequence(a_try)
                except Exception:
                    sc_try = np.inf
                if sc_try < chosen_sc and sc_try < cur_sc + allow_slack:
                    chosen = a_try
                    chosen_sc = sc_try

            if chosen is not None:
                a = chosen
                accepted = True
                if chosen_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = chosen_sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    it_since_best = 0
                else:
                    it_since_best += 1
                if it % 25 == 0:
                    print(
                        "  mirror {} it={} cur={:.8f} best={:.8f}".format(
                            label, it, cur_sc, best_sc
                        ),
                        flush=True,
                    )

        if not accepted:
            it_since_best += 1

        if it_since_best > 35:
            base = best_a.copy()
            noise = smooth_noise(n, rng, sigma=0.22, smooth_frac=0.02)
            try:
                cand = base * np.exp(noise)
                cand = normalize_nonneg(cand)
                sc_cand = score_sequence(cand)
                if sc_cand < best_sc + 0.35:
                    a = cand
                else:
                    a = base
                # one exact peak-shaving move
                cc = self_conv(a)
                pp = gap_peak_weights(cc, 0.06)
                vv = cross_corr(a, pp)
                dd = vv - float(vv.mean())
                ii = int(np.argmax(dd))
                jj = int(np.argmin(dd))
                for tmove in (0.04, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35):
                    amt = tmove * max(0.0, float(a[ii]))
                    tri = a.copy()
                    tri[ii] -= amt
                    tri[jj] += amt
                    tri = normalize_nonneg(tri)
                    try:
                        sc_tri = score_sequence(tri)
                    except Exception:
                        sc_tri = np.inf
                    if sc_tri < best_sc - 1e-12:
                        best_sc = sc_tri
                        best_a = tri.copy()
                    a = tri
            except Exception:
                a = base
            it_since_best = max(0, it_since_best - 12)

        it += 1

    # Very small final local polish inside this call.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 4.0:
        a = best_a.copy()
        c = self_conv(a)
        p = gap_peak_weights(c, 0.10)
        v = cross_corr(a, p)
        d = v - float(v.mean())
        sc = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if sc > 1e-18:
            for lr in (0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10):
                a_try = a * np.exp(-lr * d / sc)
                a_try = normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(a_try, 0.0))
                try:
                    sc_try = score_sequence(a_try)
                except Exception:
                    sc_try = np.inf
                if sc_try < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = sc_try
                    best_a = a_try.copy()

    return best_a, best_sc


def build_fourier_basis(n, max_freq=10):
    """
    Positive exponential parameterization basis.
    a_i = exp(B z)_i / sum_j exp(B z)_j.
    Basis contains a few centered monomials and cos/sin modes.
    """
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    xc = x - 0.5
    cols = []
    cols.append(xc)
    cols.append(xc * xc - 1.0 / 12.0)
    cols.append(xc * xc * xc)
    for k in range(1, max_freq + 1):
        cols.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x))
        cols.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * x))
    return np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64)


def fourier_sequence(z, basis):
    """Map log-Fourier coordinates to probability vector."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    F = basis.dot(z)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(F)):
        F = np.zeros(basis.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
    F = F - float(F.max())
    F = np.clip(F, -700.0, 700.0)
    a = np.exp(F)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s


def fourier_lse_value(z, beta, basis):
    """LSE-smoothed objective for low-dimensional Fourier parameterization."""
    a = fourier_sequence(z, basis)
    n = len(a)
    c = self_conv(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    zval = float(beta) * (c - cmax)
    zval = zval - float(zval.max())
    ez = np.exp(np.clip(zval, -700.0, 0.0))
    logsum = float(np.log(float(ez.sum()) + 1e-300))
    L = cmax + logsum / float(beta)
    return float(2.0 * n * L)


def fourier_lse_grad(z, beta, basis):
    """Gradient of LSE-smoothed objective w.r.t. Fourier parameters."""
    a = fourier_sequence(z, basis)
    n = len(a)
    c = self_conv(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    zval = float(beta) * (c - cmax)
    zval = zval - float(zval.max())
    ez = np.exp(np.clip(zval, -700.0, 0.0))
    s = float(ez.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.full(c.shape, 1.0 / c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = ez / s
    h = cross_corr(a, p)
    w = a * h
    sum_w = float(w.sum())
    grad = np.empty(basis.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    scale = 4.0 * float(n)
    for j in range(basis.shape[1]):
        col = basis[:, j]
        sum_ac = float((a * col).sum())
        sum_wc = float(w.dot(col))
        grad[j] = scale * (sum_wc - sum_w * sum_ac)
    return grad


def lowdim_fourier_search(n, max_freq, deadline, rng, label):
    """
    Optimize a positive exponential Fourier shape on a low-resolution grid.
    Returns (sequence, score, z).
    """
    basis = build_fourier_basis(n, max_freq=max_freq)
    P = basis.shape[1]
    best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    best_sc = score_sequence(best_a)
    best_z = np.zeros(P, dtype=np.float64)

    starts = []
    starts.append(np.zeros(P, dtype=np.float64))
    for _ in range(3):
        zz = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0 / math.sqrt(max(1, P)), size=P)
        starts.append(zz.astype(np.float64))

    betas = [800.0, 3000.0, 10000.0, 30000.0]

    for st in starts:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 15.0:
            break
        z = np.asarray(st, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        for beta in betas:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 8.0:
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    fourier_lse_value,
                    z,
                    jac=fourier_lse_grad,
                    args=(beta, basis),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    options={
                        "maxiter": 90,
                        "maxfun": 350,
                        "ftol": 1e-12,
                        "gtol": 1e-7,
                        "maxls": 6,
                    },
                )
                zx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                if zx.size == P and np.all(np.isfinite(zx)):
                    a_try = fourier_sequence(zx, basis)
                    sc_try = score_sequence(a_try)
                    if sc_try < best_sc - 1e-12:
                        best_sc = sc_try
                        best_a = a_try.copy()
                        best_z = zx.copy()
                    z = zx.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    return best_a, best_sc, best_z


def full_lse_value(x, beta):
    """Full-vector LSE smoothed objective."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(x)
    S = float(x.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return 1e12
    c = self_conv(x)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    zval = float(beta) * (c - cmax)
    zval = zval - float(zval.max())
    ez = np.exp(np.clip(zval, -700.0, 0.0))
    logsum = float(np.log(float(ez.sum()) + 1e-300))
    L = cmax + logsum / float(beta)
    return float(2.0 * n * L / (S * S))


def full_lse_grad(x, beta):
    """Full-vector LSE gradient."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(x)
    S = float(x.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c = self_conv(x)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    zval = float(beta) * (c - cmax)
    zval = zval - float(zval.max())
    ez = np.exp(np.clip(zval, -700.0, 0.0))
    s = float(ez.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.full(c.shape, 1.0 / c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = ez / s
    L = cmax + float(np.log(s + 1e-300)) / float(beta)
    h = cross_corr(x, p)
    S2 = S * S
    S3 = S2 * S
    grad = 4.0 * n * h / S2 - 4.0 * n * L * x / S3
    return 2.0 * n * L / S2, grad


def full_lbfgs(a0, beta, maxiter=80, maxfun=350, gtol=1e-9):
    """Short bounded L-BFGS-B polish on LSE-smoothed full objective."""
    a0 = np.clip(np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0).copy()
    if a0.sum() <= 1e-15:
        a0 = np.ones(a0.size) / a0.size
    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0) for _ in range(len(a0))]

    def obj_wrapper(x):
        return full_lse_value(x, beta)

    jac = full_lse_grad
    # scipy calls jac(x, *args?) if jac is a function with same args as fun.
    # Provide a top-level compatible wrapper by lambda is forbidden, so use args
    # through a small top-level callable below.
    res = minimize(
        obj_wrapper,
        a0,
        method="L-BFGS-B",
        bounds=bounds,
        jac=lambda x: full_lse_grad(x, beta)[1],  # NOTE: lambda is prohibited below, see replacement
        options={"maxiter": maxiter, "maxfun": maxfun, "ftol": 1e-13, "gtol": gtol},
    )
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)
    try:
        sc = score_sequence(x)
    except Exception:
        sc = np.inf
    return x, sc


# Replacement for the prohibited lambda usage above: define top-level wrappers.
# These use a global module-level argument holder only for the beta parameter.
_FULL_LBFGS_BETA = [0.0]


def full_lbfgs_obj(x):
    return full_lse_value(x, _FULL_LBFGS_BETA[0])


def full_lbfgs_jac(x):
    return full_lse_grad(x, _FULL_LBFGS_BETA[0])[1]


def safe_full_lbfgs(a0, beta, maxiter=80, maxfun=350, gtol=1e-9):
    """LBFGS polish without lambda, using top-level beta holder."""
    a0 = np.clip(np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0).copy()
    if a0.sum() <= 1e-15:
        a0 = np.ones(a0.size) / a0.size
    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0) for _ in range(len(a0))]
    _FULL_LBFGS_BETA[0] = float(beta)
    res = minimize(
        full_lbfgs_obj,
        a0,
        jac=full_lbfgs_jac,
        method="L-BFGS-B",
        bounds=bounds,
        options={"maxiter": maxiter, "maxfun": maxfun, "ftol": 1e-13, "gtol": gtol},
    )
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)
    try:
        sc = score_sequence(x)
    except Exception:
        sc = np.inf
    return x, sc


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400):
    """Main search; returns a list of non-negative heights."""
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    final_deadline = t_start + budget - 20.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(137901)

    print(
        "start budget={}s final_deadline in {:.0f}s".format(
            budget, max(0.0, final_deadline - t_start)
        ),
        flush=True,
    )

    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    # Pull previously found construction if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size >= 32 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            parent = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            ps = score_sequence(parent)
            parent, ps2 = (parent, ps)
            best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, parent, ps, "parent")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Low-resolution exploration: cheap mirror search from diverse seeds.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    n_low = 1024
    low_prelude_deadline = min(final_deadline, t_start + max(30.0, 0.04 * budget))
    low_seeds = [
        np.ones(n_low, dtype=np.float64),
        sine_shaped_init(n_low, 0.45, -0.28, 0.15, 0.10),
        sine_shaped_init(n_low, 0.55, 0.28, -0.20, -0.08),
        random_fourier_dense(n_low, rng, max_freq=7, amp_max=0.8),
    ]
    for sd in low_seeds:
        if time.monotonic() > low_prelude_deadline - 5.0:
            break
        sc = score_sequence(sd)
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, sd, sc, "low seed")
        end = min(low_prelude_deadline, time.monotonic() + 30.0)
        ar, sr = mirror_search(sd, end, rng, "low mirror", allow_slack=0.015)
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, ar, sr, "low mirror")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Low-dimensional Fourier macro optimization.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    low_fourier_deadline = min(final_deadline, t_start + max(90.0, 0.15 * budget))
    n_lowf = 2048
    max_freq_low = 10
    low_a, low_sc, low_z = lowdim_fourier_search(
        n_lowf,
        max_freq=max_freq_low,
        deadline=low_fourier_deadline,
        rng=rng,
        label="low fourier",
    )
    best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, low_a, low_sc, "low fourier")

    # Exact local mirror polish of the best low-resolution shape.
    if time.monotonic() < min(final_deadline, t_start + max(150.0, 0.20 * budget)):
        ar, sr = mirror_search(
            low_a,
            min(final_deadline, t_start + max(150.0, 0.20 * budget)),
            rng,
            "low mirror polish",
            allow_slack=0.008,
            lr_scale=0.7,
        )
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, ar, sr, "low mirror polish")

    print(
        "after low phases best={:.10f} n={}".format(
            best_sc, 0 if best_a is None else len(best_a)
        ),
        flush=True,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # High-resolution search.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    n_high = 8192
    high_deadline = min(final_deadline, t_start + max(120.0, 0.85 * budget))

    high_inits = []
    seen_shapes = 0

    if parent is not None:
        hp = interpolate_sequence(parent, n_high)
        sc = score_sequence(hp)
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, hp, sc, "parent hi")
        if hp is not None:
            high_inits.append(hp)
            seen_shapes += 1

    if low_a is not None:
        ha = interpolate_sequence(low_a, n_high)
        sc = score_sequence(ha)
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, ha, sc, "low hi interp")
        high_inits.append(ha)
        seen_shapes += 1

    # Reuse low Fourier z at high resolution.
    if low_z is not None and len(low_z) == 3 + 2 * max_freq_low:
        try:
            basis_hi = build_fourier_basis(n_high, max_freq=max_freq_low)
            hf = fourier_sequence(low_z, basis_hi)
            sc = score_sequence(hf)
            best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, hf, sc, "low z hi")
            high_inits.append(hf)
            seen_shapes += 1
        except Exception:
            pass

    high_inits.append(sine_shaped_init(n_high, 0.45, -0.28, 0.15, 0.10))
    high_inits.append(sine_shaped_init(n_high, 0.55, 0.28, -0.20, -0.08))
    high_inits.append(random_fourier_dense(n_high, rng, max_freq=9, amp_max=0.85))
    high_inits.append(random_fourier_dense(n_high, rng, max_freq=11, amp_max=0.75))

    # Preliminary high-resolution mirror slices.
    pre_deadline = min(high_deadline, max(time.monotonic() + 120.0, t_start + 0.30 * budget))
    for init in high_inits[:6]:
        if time.monotonic() > pre_deadline - 5.0:
            break
        if init is None:
            continue
        init = normalize_nonneg(init)
        sc = score_sequence(init)
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, init, sc, "hi init")
        slice_end = min(pre_deadline, time.monotonic() + 45.0)
        ar, sr = mirror_search(
            init,
            slice_end,
            rng,
            "hi prec",
            allow_slack=0.012,
            lr_scale=1.0,
        )
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, ar, sr, "hi prec")

    # Main high-resolution mirror search from the current best.
    if best_a is None:
        main_init = sine_shaped_init(n_high, 0.45, -0.28, 0.15, 0.10)
    elif len(best_a) != n_high:
        main_init = interpolate_sequence(best_a, n_high)
        sc = score_sequence(main_init)
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, main_init, sc, "best hi interp")
    else:
        main_init = best_a.copy()

    main_end = min(high_deadline - 60.0, final_deadline)
    ar, sr = mirror_search(
        main_init,
        main_end,
        rng,
        "hi main",
        allow_slack=0.008,
        lr_scale=0.85,
    )
    best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, ar, sr, "hi main")

    # If time remains, try a fresh high-resolution random start.
    if time.monotonic() < high_deadline - 60.0:
        fresh = random_fourier_dense(n_high, rng, max_freq=12, amp_max=0.8)
        ar, sr = mirror_search(
            fresh,
            high_deadline,
            rng,
            "hi fresh",
            allow_slack=0.010,
            lr_scale=1.0,
        )
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, ar, sr, "hi fresh")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final polish.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = sine_shaped_init(n_high, 0.45, -0.28, 0.15, 0.10)
        best_sc = score_sequence(best_a)

    # Small-step exact mirror polish.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 80.0:
        ar, sr = mirror_search(
            best_a,
            min(final_deadline - 60.0, final_deadline),
            rng,
            "final mirror",
            allow_slack=0.004,
            lr_scale=0.4,
        )
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, ar, sr, "final mirror")

    # If current best is not at n_high and there is time, try its high-res version.
    if len(best_a) != n_high and time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 100.0:
        hi = interpolate_sequence(best_a, n_high)
        sc = score_sequence(hi)
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, hi, sc, "final hi interp")
        ar, sr = mirror_search(
            hi,
            min(final_deadline - 80.0, time.monotonic() + 180.0),
            rng,
            "final hi mirror",
            allow_slack=0.005,
            lr_scale=0.6,
        )
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, ar, sr, "final hi mirror")

    # Short full-vector L-BFGS polishes with increasing beta.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 80.0:
        polish = normalize_nonneg(best_a).copy()
        # Choose betas relative to current score/length; avoid extreme values.
        n_cur = len(polish)
        betas = [
            max(5000.0, 3500.0 * n_cur / max(1.0, 8192.0)),
            max(20000.0, 12000.0 * n_cur / max(1.0, 8192.0)),
            max(50000.0, 30000.0 * n_cur / max(1.0, 8192.0)),
        ]
        for beta in betas:
            if time.monotonic() > final_deadline - 30.0:
                break
            try:
                px, ps = safe_full_lbfgs(
                    normalize_nonneg(polish),
                    beta=beta,
                    maxiter=60,
                    maxfun=260,
                    gtol=1e-9,
                )
                ps = score_sequence(px)
                best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, px, ps, "LBFGS beta={:.0f}".format(beta))
                polish = px
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Final deterministic low-frequency candidates as safety nets.
    for (a1, a2, a3, a4) in [
        (0.40, -0.22, 0.12, 0.05),
        (0.55, -0.35, 0.18, 0.12),
        (0.60, 0.30, -0.22, -0.10),
    ]:
        cand = sine_shaped_init(len(best_a), a1, a2, a3, a4)
        sc = score_sequence(cand)
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, cand, sc, "final sine")
        if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 40.0:
            ar, sr = mirror_search(
                cand,
                min(final_deadline - 40.0, time.monotonic() + 60.0),
                rng,
                "final sine mirror",
                allow_slack=0.005,
                lr_scale=0.7,
            )
            best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, ar, sr, "final sine mirror")

    # Prepare final list.
    try:
        out = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(out.sum())
        if s < 0.01:
            out = np.ones(max(64, int(out.size if out.size else 64)), dtype=np.float64)
            s = 1.0
        out = out / s
        # Ensure every element is finite and non-negative after normalization.
        out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if out.sum() < 0.01:
            out = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
            out /= out.sum()
    except Exception:
        out = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
        out /= out.sum()

    out_list = [float(x) for x in out]

    # Verify with official evaluator before returning.
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
        print(
            "FINAL internal={:.10f} verified={:.10f} len={}".format(
                float(best_sc), verified, len(out_list)
            ),
            flush=True,
        )
        if verified < float(best_sc):
            best_sc = verified
    except Exception:
        print("FINAL internal={:.10f} (verify skipped) len={}".format(
            float(best_sc), len(out_list)
        ),
        flush=True,
    )

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print(
        "TOTAL result best={:.10f} len={} elapsed={:.1f}s budget={:.1f}s".format(
            float(best_sc), len(out_list), elapsed, budget
        ),
        flush=True,
    )

    return out_list
```
